# Probe audit — shipped training circuits

## Method

Scanned the 10,200 shipped `training-data/**/solution.sop` circuits (the 9,495 training rows plus the 705 `eval/` holdout rows), extracted every `probe(condition, "message")` call — 54,155 probes across 2,291 distinct message texts — and classified each into the five contract classes. Ambiguous cases were resolved against the generating family (`teacher/procedural/*.mjs` and `teacher/families/**/*.mjs`): a probe was judged a false guard when the family's own oracle (`solve`/`render`) accepts the 0 / empty / tie answer the probe rejects, and a genuine invariant when the oracle itself enforces the property (throws on the bad case). Locations below are the shipped family directory (the level above each `solution.sop`). Read-only audit; nothing was modified.

## Counts

| Class | Probes | Distinct texts |
|---|---|---|
| 1 — Parameter check | 40,200 | 1,905 |
| 2 — Disconnected boilerplate | 960 | 27 |
| 3 — Genuine answer invariant (keep) | 11,805 | 328 |
| 4 — False guard | 800 | 18 |
| 5 — Honest case (keep) | 390 | 13 |
| **Total** | **54,155** | **2,291** |

## Note on removal

Removing the three bad classes (1, 2, 4) deletes 41,960 of the 54,155 probe statements — 77.5% of probe calls and about 5.0 MB of the 6.3 MB of probe-line text (~79.5% of probe characters, on the order of 1.26 M tokens at 4 characters/token), or ~31.7% of the total shipped `solution.sop` character count. That is a sizing estimate of the probe lines only, not a prediction of the trained-target change. Any such change is a dataset regeneration to be performed only after the running exp-017 evaluation chain closes; this audit changes nothing in the tree.
